<script type="text/javascript">
        (function () {
            if (!$) {
                return;
            }
            if (!MZCommons) {
                return;
            }

            function getCurrentOverheadCoupon() {
                var couponSchedule = [{"coupon_id":1116425,"start_time":1571756400,"end_time":1571842740,"pc_banner":null,"banner_css":null,"sp_banner":null,"ebook_enabled":true,"book_enabled":false,"coupon_image":null,"coupon_price_str":"300","product_lower_price":3000,"coupon_code":"ohqia300"}];
                var nowTime = new Date().getTime() / 1000;
                for (var i = 0; i < couponSchedule.length; i++) {
                    var couponData = couponSchedule[i];
                    if (couponData['start_time'] <= nowTime
                        && nowTime < couponData['end_time']) {
                        return couponData;
                    }
                }
                return null
            }

            var overheadCoupon = getCurrentOverheadCoupon();

            var container = $('<div>', {
                addClass: "row overhead-pr-row justify-content-center"
            });
            var overheadPr = $('<div>', {
                addClass: "overhead-pr",
                html: container
            });

            function makeWelcomePointDiv() {
                if (MZCommons.loggedIn()) {
                    return null;
                }
                var welcomePoint = 0;
                if (!welcomePoint) {
                    return null;
                }
                var container = $('<a>', {
                    addClass: 'welcome-point-container col-12 col-sm-6',
                    click: function () {
                        ga('send', 'event', 'overhead-point-present',
                            'click-link', welcomePoint, null,
                            {nonInteraction: 1});
                    },
                    html: [
                        $('<div>', {
                            addClass: 'row pr-row',
                            html: [
                                // $('<div>', {
                                //     html: '5/31<br>まで',
                                //    addClass: 'col-2 col-l'
                                // }),
                                $('<div>', {
                                    html: '<strong>' + welcomePoint +
                                    '</strong>ポイント',
                                    addClass: 'col-7 col-c'
                                }),
                                $('<div>', {
                                    html: '会員登録で<br>プレゼント',
                                    addClass: 'col-3 col-r'
                                })
                            ]
                        })
                    ]
                });
                setTimeout(function () {
                    container.attr('href', '/entry/kiyaku.php');
                }, 2000);
                return container;
            }

            function makeCouponDiv() {
                if (!overheadCoupon) {
                    return null;
                }
                var endDate = new Date(overheadCoupon.end_time * 1000);
                var container = $('<a />', {
                    attr: {
                        href: "javascript:void(0);"
                    },
                    addClass: 'coupon-container col-12 col-sm-6',
                    html: [
                        $('<div>', {
                            addClass: 'row pr-row',
                            html: [
                                $('<div>', {
                                    html: '' + (endDate.getMonth() + 1) + '/'
                                    + endDate.getDate() + '<br>まで',
                                    addClass: 'col-2 col-l'
                                }),
                                (function () {
                                    if (overheadCoupon.coupon_id === 1065150
                                      || overheadCoupon.coupon_id === 1065151) {
                                        return $('<div>', {
                                            html: '期間中何度でも' + overheadCoupon.coupon_price_str + '円割引',
                                            addClass: 'col-7 col-c',
                                            css: {
                                                'font-size': '16px',
                                            },
                                        });
                                    } else {
                                        return $('<div>', {
                                            html: '<small>￥</small><strong>' +
                                              overheadCoupon.coupon_price_str +
                                              '</strong>割引',
                                            addClass: 'col-7 col-c'
                                        });
                                    }
                                })(),
                                $('<div>', {
                                    html: '電子書籍<br>クーポン',
                                    addClass: 'col-3 col-r'
                                })
                            ]
                        })
                    ]

                });
                setTimeout(function () {
                    container.on('click', function () {
                        $.ajax({
                            url: "/parts/overhead_pr_popup.php",
                            success: function (data) {
                                bootbox.alert({
                                    message: data
                                });
                            }
                        });
                    });
                }, 2000);

                return container;
            }

            var wpDiv = makeWelcomePointDiv();
            var couponDiv = makeCouponDiv();
            if (!wpDiv && !couponDiv) {
                return;
            }

            var offset = 0;
            if (couponDiv) {
                container.append(couponDiv);
                offset += 50;
                if (!wpDiv) {
                    overheadPr.addClass("coupon-only");
                }
            }
            if (wpDiv) {
                container.append(wpDiv);
                offset += 50;
                if (!couponDiv) {
                    overheadPr.addClass("welcome-point-only");
                }
            }

            if (MZCommons.media.isSP()) {
                var topNav = $('#top-nav');
                $('body').css({'padding-top': topNav.height() + offset});
                topNav.prepend(overheadPr).addClass('with-overhead-pr');
            } else {
                //overheadPrImg.prop('src', overheadCoupon['pc_banner']);
                $('body').prepend(overheadPr);
            }

            // クーポンを適用して購入
            $(function () {
                if (!overheadCoupon) {
                    return;
                }
                if (!overheadCoupon.ebook_enabled) {
                    return;
                }
                var $btnForms = $('.cart-in-with-coupon-ebook');
                if (!$btnForms.length) {
                    return;
                }
                $btnForms.each(function (i, form) {
                    var $form = $(form);
                    if (overheadCoupon.product_lower_price > $form.data('price')) {
                        return true;  // クーポン適用しない商品
                    }
                    $form.find('span.coupon-price').text(overheadCoupon.coupon_price_str);
                    $form.find('input[name="coupon_code"]').val(overheadCoupon.coupon_code);

                    // GAイベント
                    $form.submit(function(){
                        var eventValue = $('h1').text();
                        ga('send', 'event', 'overhead-coupon', 'product-detail-cart-in',
                            overheadCoupon.coupon_code, eventValue);
                        return true;
                    });

                    $form.removeClass('d-none');
                })
            });

        })();
    </script>

<script>
    $(function () {
        var banners = [{"img":"\/user_data\/img\/banner\/201910sonokisekae1600838_top_b.jpg","txt":"\u305d\u306e\u7740\u305b\u66ff\u3048\u4eba\u5f62\u306f\u604b\u3092\u3059\u308b4\u5dfb\u767a\u58f2\u8a18\u5ff5\uff011\u5dfb\u7121\u6599","url":"\/special\/2198.html","visitedPath":"\/special\/2198.html","backgroundColor":"#fcfcfc","cdnResize":true}];
        // ランダム
        // var banner = MZCommons.visitStorage.choiceRandom(banners);
        // サイクル
        var banner = MZCommons.visitStorage.choiceCycle(banners);
        var imgSrc;

        if (banner.cdnResize) {
            imgSrc = MZCommons.cdnUrl(banner.img, {
                width: 1200,
                quality: 70
            });
        } else {
            imgSrc = MZCommons.cdnUrl(banner.img);
        }

        var siteHero = $('#site-hero');

        if (banner.boxClass) {
            siteHero.removeClass("w1600h838").addClass(banner.boxClass);
        }

        if (banner.backgroundColor) {
            siteHero.css('background-color', banner.backgroundColor);
        }
        if (banner.backgroundImage) {
            if (!MZCommons.device.isSmartphone()) {
                siteHero.css('background-image', 'url(' + banner.backgroundImage + ')');
            }
        }
        siteHero.html(
            $('<a />', {
                click: function() {
                    ga('send', 'event', 'site-hero', 'click-link', banner.txt)
                },
                attr: {
                    href: banner.url,
                    target: banner.target || "_self"
                },
                html: $('<img />', {
                    attr: {
                        src: imgSrc,
                        alt: banner.txt,
                    },
                    addClass: banner.imgClass || ''
                })
            })
        );
        ga('send', 'event', 'site-hero', 'impression', banner.txt);

    });
</script>

</div><div class="section-container scheme-products" style="display: none;"><div class="top-section-header"><h2 class="top-section-header-title">あなたにオススメのコミック</h2></div>
<!-- ブックカバーカルーセル JSON用 (recommend) -->
<div class="mz-carousel-container" data-bcc-name="recommend" data-category="recommend-top" data-json-url="" data-more-page-label="" data-more-page-url="/contents/new_sale.php" data-url-cdn-cache-seconds="300" id="mz-carousel-container-recommend">
<div class="book-cover-carousel" id="book-cover-carousel-recommend"><ul class="book-cover-list"></ul></div><div class="bcc-pager" id="bcc-recommend-pager"></div><div class="prev-button-area"><a aria-hidden="true" class="carousel-prev-button fa fa-chevron-left"></a></div><div class="next-button-area"><a aria-hidden="true" class="carousel-next-button fa fa-chevron-right"></a></div>
</div>

<script style="" type="text/javascript">
    $(function () {
        var d = new $.Deferred();
        $.ajax({
            url: '//www.mangazenkan.com/json/recently_recommend_products.php',
            method: 'get'
        }).then(function (allData) {
            var bccNames = [
                'recently', 'recommend'
            ];
            $.each(bccNames, function (i, v) {
                var mccId = "mz-carousel-container-" + v;
                var mcc = $('#' + mccId);
                var data = allData[v];

                if (!data.length) {
                    // データ無し。セクションコンテナを消す
                    mcc.parents('.section-container').hide();
                    return;
                }
                var action = mcc.data('action');
                var ulList = mcc.find('.book-cover-list');
                $.each(data, function (i, item) {
                    // v4.js
                    item.action = action;
                    var cell = MZCommons.makeBookCoverCell(item);
                    ulList.append(cell);
                });
                d.resolve();
            });
            MZCommons.bccDeferreds.push(d.promise());
        });
    });
</script><div class="section-container"><div class="top-section-header book"><a href="/ranking/weekly-book/"><h2 class="top-section-header-title with-more">週間ランキング[紙]</h2><div class="more-link"><span class="more-link-text">もっと見る</span><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-chevron-right"></i></div></a></div>
